___________    _____________________________________* MAX985         Single, Push/Pull* MAX986         Single, Open-Drain* MAX989         Dual, Push/Pull* MAX990         Dual, Open-Drain* MAX993 hi,lo.RH1 3 51 1E11RH2 4 51 1E11DP1 51 52 DPDP2 53 51 DPVP1 52 0 1VVP2 53 0 -1V***=================IHYST 55 0 -1.5E-9*GENERATES 3MV OF HYST.RREF 55 0 1E6*LOGIC OUTPUT, NODE 60 ALTERS THE POLARITY, SO 55 SHOULD ALWAYS BE POS.GMULT 60 0 POLY(2) 51 0 55 0 0 0  0 0 1E-6RMULT 60 0 1E6*================*EH 3 98 3 4 0.5VVIRTUAL 98 0 0VF5 3 38 VA8 1D9 40 38 DXD10 38 3 DXVA7 3 40 0F6 MODELS USED ==***.MODEL DX2 D(IS=1E-15 n=0.001) *.MODEL DX D(IS=1E-15) .ends     *****   ////////////// MAX986 MACROMODEL //////////////////**   ====>      REFER TO MAX985 DATA SHEET
